## 6. Non-functional requirements

"Gate" means a failing measurement fails the named make target. "Recorded" means `make perf` measures and publishes the figure in its report but does not fail on it [J: only G9 figures gate the loop; other figures guide tuning, U-05].

| Id | Category | Requirement | Measure | Gate or recorded | Source |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| NFR-01 | Performance | A full monthly close (schedules, journal run, rollforward) on the volume tenant (10,000 contracts, 50,000 obligations, 24 months) completes in ≤ 10 minutes locally | `make perf`: the month-24 close in a sandbox copy of tenant `perf-volume` (database `erev`), built through month 23 by `make perf-seed` | Gate (G9) | REQ-CLS-014; REQ-OPS-009; D-75 Q7 |
| NFR-02 | Performance | Contract workbench API endpoints (contract header and KPI strip, obligations list, allocation walk, schedule by period, history list) have p95 ≤ 300 ms at volume | `make perf` | Gate (G9) | REQ-OPS-008 |
| NFR-03 | Performance | Route change to rendered primary content ≤ 1.5 s p75 for SF-01, SF-02, SF-03, SF-04, SF-05 and SF-12 on the volume tenant (production build, warm cache, Chromium) | Playwright timing script | Recorded | [J] |
| NFR-04 | Performance | Validation and dry-run diff of a 50,000-row modern CSV ≤ 3 minutes | `make perf` | Recorded | REQ-SEC-012 |
| NFR-05 | Performance | One standard report for one entity and period at volume ≤ 60 seconds; larger exports stream asynchronously | `make perf` | Recorded | REQ-RPT-024 |
| NFR-06 | Performance | Period evidence pack at volume ≤ 5 minutes | `make perf` | Recorded | REQ-RPT-014 |
| NFR-07 | Performance | Audit chain verification of 1,000,000 events ≤ 10 minutes | `make perf` | Recorded | REQ-PLT-020 |
| NFR-08 | Performance | Seeding every demo tenant ≤ 15 minutes | seed timer | Recorded | WLD-R-08 |
| NFR-09 | Resilience | An AI provider call times out after 60 seconds; the proposal becomes `failed` (E-74) and the UI stays responsive | unit test with the fake provider | Gate (`make ci`) | REQ-AI-010 |
| NFR-10 | Availability | Hosted availability objective 99.5% per calendar month for API and web, excluding announced maintenance; stated in the runbook | document | Documented (no deployment in 1.0, G§4) | [J] |
| NFR-11 | Recovery | RPO ≤ 15 minutes via point-in-time recovery; RTO ≤ 4 hours; a quarterly automated restore verification of the latest production backup into an isolated instance, and an annual full DR exercise, including cutover in staging, that measures RTO | runbook; restore test records (`docs/05-ARCHITECTURE.md` OPR-12) | Documented | REQ-OPS-013; B1-035 (rev 1.1) |
| NFR-12 | Availability | No planned maintenance on business days 1 to 5 of a month, the typical close window | runbook | Documented | [J] |
| NFR-13 | Resilience | Jobs without a heartbeat for 10 minutes are marked failed, retried with backoff, dead-lettered after the limit, and notified (NTF-05) | unit test | Gate | REQ-OPS-006 |
| NFR-14 | Resilience | A failed close run resumes from its failed step without recomputing succeeded steps | unit test | Gate | REQ-CLS-012 |
| NFR-15 | Resilience | Adapter calls retry 429 and 5xx responses with exponential backoff, at most 5 attempts, without duplicate effects | adapter contract tests | Gate | REQ-INT-002, 003 |
| NFR-20 | Accessibility | WCAG 2.2 AA; zero serious or critical axe violations on every surface in light and dark themes | `make e2e` (E2E-09) | Gate (G8) | REQ-UX-007; DS-A11Y-01 |
| NFR-21 | Accessibility | Keyboard-only variants of J-04.1 to J-04.3 and J-16.6 complete | `make e2e` | Gate | DS-A11Y-10 |
| NFR-22 | Accessibility | The SF-03 obligations grid and the SF-04 waterfall expose row and column headers in the accessibility tree | Playwright accessibility snapshot | Gate | DS-A11Y-11 |
| NFR-23 | Accessibility | SF-01, SF-12 and SF-22 work at 200% text zoom and 320 CSS px width without loss of content (grids exempt) | `make e2e` | Gate | DS-A11Y-06 |
| NFR-24 | Accessibility | `prefers-reduced-motion` disables non-essential motion | `make e2e` | Gate | DS-A11Y-14 |
| NFR-30 | Browsers | Supported: the latest two stable major versions of Chrome, Edge, Firefox and Safari on desktop | release notes | Documented | [J] |
| NFR-31 | Browsers | `make e2e` runs every journey in Chromium, and J-13 and J-16 in Firefox and WebKit | `make e2e` | Gate | [J] Cross-engine coverage at bounded cost |
| NFR-32 | Viewport | Designed for ≥ 1280 × 800. From 1024 to 1279 px the rail collapses and panes stack. Below 1024 px, SF-01, SF-12 and SF-22 remain usable and other surfaces show "This screen needs a wider window." | `make e2e` viewport test | Gate | [J] Accounting workbenches are desktop tools |
| NFR-33 | Privacy | The browser makes no third-party network requests (fonts vendored, no CDN, no telemetry) | Playwright network log | Gate | REQ-SEC-008; DS-TYP-05 |
| NFR-40 | Localization | UI language English; numbers and currencies per user locale through the format module; dates `DD MMM YYYY`; timestamps UTC | unit tests | Gate | REQ-UX-022; DS-FMT-16, 17 |
| NFR-41 | Time | Effective dates are entity-local calendar dates; `recorded_at` is UTC; API timestamps convert to entity-local dates before period assignment | unit tests | Gate | REQ-CLS-005 |
| NFR-42 | Sessions | Idle timeout default 30 minutes (tenant setting 5 to 240); a warning 2 minutes before expiry offers "Stay signed in"; absolute timeout 12 hours | `make e2e` with a shortened test timeout | Gate | REQ-PLT-004 |
| NFR-43 | Security | Login and MFA endpoints are rate-limited; 5 consecutive failures lock the account for 15 minutes | unit tests | Gate | REQ-PLT-004; REQ-SEC-004 |
| NFR-50 | Data limits | Uploads within the per-purpose limits of 04 T-PLT-29: attachments and SSP studies 25 MiB; import sources 50 MiB and at most 50,000 data rows per sheet; legacy databases 500 MiB. A file over its purpose limit or of a disallowed media type is refused with ERR-37; a sheet above 50,000 rows raises `IMPORT_ROW_LIMIT_EXCEEDED` (IMP-42) and the import becomes `INVALID` | unit tests | Gate | REQ-SEC-012; T-PLT-29; D-75 Q10 (B1-016) |
| NFR-51 | Determinism | Recomputing a contract version from its events and pinned reference versions reproduces its stored output hash; Explain "Verify" exposes the check (J-16.3) | properties; `make e2e` | Gate | D-10; REQ-RPT-018 |
| NFR-52 | Determinism | Re-running a report from the same snapshot reproduces totals and output hash | J-15.6 | Gate | REQ-RPT-002; CTL-029 |

## 7. Legacy-user transition (M-DES-07)

### 7.1 The 14 legacy buttons

Legacy button labels, groups and behaviour are facts from legacy 01 §1.1 and §1.5 [F].

| Id | Legacy group | Legacy button | Legacy behaviour | eRev Cloud feature | Where | What changes for the user | REQ | Journey |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| LTM-01 | Contract Operations | Load SSPs | Replaced the whole `SKU_SSP` table on every upload | "Legacy v1: SKU SSP" import creating an SSP book version submitted for approval; versions append | SF-10, SF-13 | SSPs need an approver before use; history is kept; no silent replacement | REQ-SSP-001, 007, 013; REQ-DAT-002 | J-01 |
| LTM-02 | Contract Operations | Load Contracts | Appended setup rows and allocated immediately | "Legacy v1: Contract Setup" import with dry-run allocation walk; approval activates | SF-10, SF-03 | Allocation is visible before commit; re-uploading an existing contract is refused (IMP-16) | REQ-DAT-002, 015, 017; REQ-CON-005 | J-01 |
| LTM-03 | Contract Operations | Load Delivery and Billing | Applied deliveries and billings at a typed date; dropped blank-memo rows | "Legacy v1: Contract Progress Tracking" import (mode "Delivery and billing", effective date), modern CSV, or integrations | SF-10 | Duplicate rows are summed with a notice; blank memos warn instead of dropping rows; over-delivery names the obligation; late dates post to the first open period | REQ-REC-024, 025; REQ-CLS-004 | J-01, J-04 |
| LTM-04 | Mod Operations | Prospective Contract Mod | Pooled reallocation over remaining SSP | "Legacy v1: Contract Modification" with mode `prospective`, or the modification wizard | SF-10, SF-07 | The system proposes the treatment and shows an impact preview; approval required | REQ-DAT-003; REQ-MOD-002, 007, 013 | J-05, J-21 |
| LTM-05 | Mod Operations | Retrospective Contract Mod | Full retrospective re-allocation with catch-ups | Mode `retrospective` (preset) or the wizard's cumulative catch-up treatment | SF-10, SF-07 | Same as LTM-04 | REQ-MOD-005, 008 | J-06, J-21 |
| LTM-06 | Mod Operations | POB Specific VC | Added a price change to one obligation with a catch-up | Mode `pob_price_change` or a price change on the obligation | SF-10, SF-03 | Quantity must be 0; VC elements cannot be targeted | REQ-TP-007 | J-21 |
| LTM-07 | Journals and Reporting | Revenue Journal Entries | Excel gross and delta JE reports for a date range, sometimes 0.01 out of balance | Journal runs per entity, book and period with approval, export and acknowledgement; gross and adjustment date-range views | SF-06 | Every batch balances; exports go to CSV, NetSuite or QuickBooks Online adapters | REQ-JE-001, 007, 008, 011, 014, 015 | J-01, J-13, J-21 |
| LTM-08 | Journals and Reporting | Contract History | Excel of all versions in a date range | "Contract history" report (grid and the 71-column legacy export); workbench History tab | SF-08, SF-03 | Parameterized; a start date after the end date is refused | REQ-RPT-012 | J-15, J-21 |
| LTM-09 | Journals and Reporting | Latest Contract Status | Excel of the latest version per POB | "Latest contract status" report with the legacy export; workbench KPI strip | SF-08, SF-03 | Run record with hash for each export | REQ-RPT-013 | J-21 |
| LTM-10 | Premium Database Functions | Backup Database | Copied the database file to one backup slot | "Create sandbox copy" | SF-15:sandbox | The copy is a sandbox workspace you can open; production is untouched | REQ-PLT-023 | J-25 |
| LTM-11 | Premium Database Functions | Restore from Backup | Deleted the live database and copied the backup over it | "Restore into a new sandbox" creates a new sandbox from a stored snapshot; corrections in production use void, reversal or reopen | SF-15:sandbox | Production data is never overwritten | REQ-PLT-024 | J-25 |
| LTM-12 | Premium Database Functions | !Reset Database Completely! | Dropped every table | "Reset sandbox" (sandbox workspaces only) | SF-15:sandbox | Refused for production (ERR-25) | REQ-PLT-025 | J-25 |
| LTM-13 | Premium Database Functions | Append from Another | Positional insert of another database's rows without de-duplication | Legacy database import (opening balances) or template replay, each with reconciliation and approval | SF-19 | Duplicates are refused; differences are reconciled before promotion | REQ-MIG-001 to 004 | J-20, J-21 |
| LTM-14 | Premium Database Functions | Purge Contracts | Deleted rows by name and date range | "Void contract" with approval; reversal lines in the first open period | SF-03 | Nothing is deleted; history stays | REQ-CON-015 | J-26 |

### 7.3 Other legacy behaviours retired

| Legacy behaviour [F] | eRev Cloud replacement |
|---|---|
| Licence key dialog and premium gating (legacy 01 §1.2) | Removed; every feature is standard (D-01, REQ-SEC-009) |
| End-of-life shutdown on 2025-12-31 (legacy 01 §1.1) | Removed (REQ-SEC-009) |
| External links and background images in the window | Removed; the About dialog (SF-27) shows "eRev by Chipmunk Robotics" and `Copyright (c) 2025-2026 ChipmunkRPA` (D-61; D-75); no outbound requests (REQ-SEC-008) |
| Success and error popups | Toasts for outcomes, findings in the dry-run diff, exception items and notifications (§5.4, §5.5) |
| Typed "Current Period" date prompt | Effective date in the import wizard; context pill for reads (BR-UX-02) |
| Excel files overwritten in the application folder | Downloads with report run records and hashes (REQ-RPT-002) |
| `Processing Time Log` naive local timestamp | `recorded_at` UTC and the audit log (REQ-PLT-018) |
| Forecasting by copying the application folder and re-running templates | Scenario tenants with forecast event sets (J-18) |
| Direct SQLite access for BI tools | CSV extracts with manifests, including the 71-column dataset (REQ-RPT-028) |

REQ-DEMO-005 is amended; §2.3 already conforms. |
